mˈoxutsʰi da zɣvˈa 

ˈis ˈiqo mˈoxutsʰi kˈatsʰi rˈomelitsʰ mˈarto tʰˈevzaˌobda ɡˈolpʰstrimʃi skˈipʰʃi da ˈis 
ˈukve ˈotʰxmotsʰdˌatʰotʰxmˌeti dɣˈe ˈiqo ɡˈasuli tʰˈevzis ɡˈareʃe pˈirvelʃi 
ˈormotsʰi dɣˈe ˈiqo mˈastʰan bˈici mˈaɡram ˈormotsʰi dɣˈis ʃˈemdeɡ ɡˈareʃe 
tʰˈevzi bˈicis mʃˈoblebma ˈutʰxres rˈom mˈoxutsʰi ˈaxla nˈamdvilad ˈiqo 
da bˈolos sˈalao rˈomelitsʰ ˈuiɣblˌobis qvˈelaze tsʰˈudi pʰˈormaa da bˈici 
mˈatʰi brdzˈanebitʰ tsˈavida sxvˈa nˈavitʰ rˈomelmatsʰ sˈami kˈarɡi tʰˈevzi dˈaicˌira 
pˈirveli kvˈira bˈics sˈevdiˌani mˈohqva tʰˈitʰoˌeulʃi mˈoxutsʰis dˈanaxva 
dɣˈe mˈisi skˈipʰi tsʰˈariˌeli ˈiqo da ˈis qˈoveltʰvis tʃˈamodˌioda mˈis dˈasaxmˌareblad 
ˈan dˈaxveˌuli xˈazebi ˈan ɡˈapʰi da hˈarpuni da ˈialkʰˌani rˈom ˈiqo 
ˈandzis ˈirɡvliv ɡˈaʃlili ˈialkʰˌani pʰkʰvˈilis tˈomrebitʰ ˈiqo ɡˈakruli da 
ɡˈabrətsqinˌebuli mˈudmivi dˈamartsʰxˌebis drˈoʃas hɡˈavda 

mˈoxutsʰi ˈiqo ɡˈamxdari da ɡˈapʰitʰrˌebuli ɣrmˈa nˈaocˌebitʰ mˈis ˈukan 
kˈiseri kˈanis kˈetʰilɡˌantsqobˌili kˈibos qˈavispʰˌeri lˈakʰebi mzˈes mˈoakʰvs 
trˈopikul zɣvˈaze mˈisi ˈanarˌeklidan lˈoqebze ˈiqo lˈakʰebi 
kˈarɡad ˈeʃvebˌoda sˈaxis ɡvˈerdebze da xˈelebi ɣrmˈad hkʰˈonda nˈaketsʰˌebi 
nˈatsibˌurebi dzˈapʰebze mdzˈime tʰˈevzis tˈarebˌisɡan mˈaɡram ˈartsʰertʰi ˈes nˈatsibˌurebi 
ˈaxali ˈiqo ˈisini ˈisetʰˌive dzvˈeli ˈiqvnen rˈoɡortsʰ ˈerozˌia ˈutʰevzod ˈudabnˌoʃi 

mˈasze qvˈelapʰˌeri dzvˈeli ˈiqo tʰvˈalebis ɡˈarda da ˈiɡive ˈiqo 
pʰˈeri rˈoɡortsʰ zɣvˈa da ˈiqo mxˈiarˌuli da dˈaumˌartsʰxebˌeli 

sˈantiˌaɡo ˈutʰxra bˈicma rˈotsʰa ˈisini ˈavidnen bˈankze sˈaidˌanatsʰ ˈiqo 
skˈipʰi ˈastsies mˈe ʃˈemedzlo ˈisev ʃˈentʰan ˈertʰad tsˈavide tʃvˈen mˈovamzˌadetʰ 
pʰˈuli 

mˈoxutsʰma bˈics tʰˈevzaˌoba ˈastsavla da bˈics ˈuqvarda 

ˈara tʰkʰvˈa mˈoxutsʰma ˈiɣbliˌani nˈavitʰ xˈar dˈartʃi mˈatʰtʰan 

mˈaɡram dˈaimˌaxsovre rˈoɡor ɡˈaiˌaretʰ ˈotʰxmotsʰdˌaʃvidi dɣˈe tʰˈevzis ɡˈareʃe da ʃˈemdeɡ tʃvˈen 
sˈami kvˈiris ɡˈanmavlˌobaʃi qˈoveldɣe ˈicerda dˈidebs

tʰkʰvˈen ˈecvi ɡˈeparˌebodatʰ 

ˈes ˈiqo mˈamam mˈaidzˌula tsˈavsulˌiqavi mˈe bˈici vˈar da mˈas ˈunda dˈavemˌortʃilo 

vˈitsʰi tʰkʰvˈa mˈoxutsʰma ˈes sˈakmaod nˈormalˌuria 

mˈas ˈar ˈakʰvs dˈidi rətsmˈena 

ˈara tʰkʰvˈa mˈoxutsʰma mˈaɡram ɡvˈakʰvs ˈara 

dˈiax tʰkʰvˈa bˈicma ʃˈeidzlˌeba lˈudi ʃˈemoɡtʰˌavazotʰ tˈerasˌaze da mˈere 
nˈivtʰebs sˈaxlʃi tsˈaviqvantʰ

ˈisxdnen tˈerasˌaze da bˈevri mˈetʰevze dˈastsʰinˌoda dzvˈelebs

stˈabilˌurad kˈarɡi ˈamindi da rˈatsʰ nˈaxes tsˈarmatˌebuli 
ˈim dɣˈis mˈetʰevzˌeebi ˈukve ˈiqvnen da dˈaxotsʰes mˈarlini

ʃˈemadzrtsˌunebˌeli qˈoveli pʰˈitsʰris bˈolos tʰˈevzis sˈaxlamde sˈadatsʰ ˈisini 
dˈaelˌoda qˈinulis sˈatvirtʰo mˈankʰanas rˈomelitsʰ mˈatʰ hˈavanˌaʃi bˈazarʃi ɡˈadaˌitana ˈimatʰ 
rˈomelmatsʰ zvˈiɡenˌebi dˈaicˌira mˈeore mxˈares zvˈiɡenˌebis kʰˈarxanˌaʃi tsˈaiqvˌana 
mxˈares (en)kˈəʊv(ka) sˈadatsʰ ˈisini (en)hˈɔɪstɪd ˌɒn(ka) blˈoki da dˈadzleva mˈatʰi 
ɣvˈidzli ˈamoˌiɣes pʰˈarpʰlebi mˈoacres da tqˈavi mˈoaʃˌores da 
mˈatʰi xˈortsʰi mˈocrili ʃˈevida zˈolebi dˈasamˌarilˌeblad 

rˈotsʰa ˈaɣmosˌavletʰis kʰˈari ˈiqo nˈavsadɡˌurʃi sˈuni mˈovida 
zvˈiɡenis kʰˈarxana mˈaɡram dɣˈes sˈunis mxˈolod sˈusti zɣvˈari ˈiqo 
rˈadɡan kʰˈari tʃrdˈiloˌetʰisken ɡˈabrunda da ʃˈemdeɡ tʃˈamovˌarda da ˈis 
sˈasiˌamovno da mzˈiani ˈiqo tˈerasˌaze 

sˈantiˌaɡo tʰkʰvˈa bˈicma 

dˈiax tʰkʰvˈa mˈoxutsʰma cˈikʰas ˈecira da bˈevrze pʰˈikʰrobda 
tslˈebis tsˈin

ˈara tsˈadi da ˈitʰamˌaʃe bˈeisbˌoli mˈe mˈaintsʰ ʃˈemidzlˌia nˈitʃbosnˌoba da rˈoxelˌio tʃˈaaɡdebs

da mˈan kˈinaɣam nˈavi dˈaanɡrˌia ɡˈaxsovs 

mˈaxsovs kˈudis dˈartqma da dˈartqma da tʃˈaʃla 
da klˈubis xmˈauri mˈaxsovs rˈom ʃˈen mˈe tʃˈamaɡde

mˈaxsovs qvˈelapʰˌeri rˈotsʰa pˈirvelad tsˈaveditʰ ˈertʰad 

mˈoxutsʰma ʃˈexeda mˈas mzˈisɡan dˈamtsvari tʰˈavdadʒˌerebˌuli mˈosiqvˌarule tʰvˈalebitʰ 

tʃˈemi bˈici rˈom ˈiqo ɡˈamoɡˌiqvandi da ˈazartul tʰˈamaʃs vˈitʰamˌaʃebdi tʰkʰvˈa mˈan mˈaɡram ʃˈen

ˈori tʰkʰvˈa bˈicma

ˈar tʃkʰˈarobda da tʰˈavis xˈazebs mˈaɣla da kʰvˈevitʰ ˈinaxˌavda mˈaɡram ˈis

tʃˈiti 

tʃˈiti hˈaerʃi ˈupʰro mˈaɣla ˈavida da ˈisev ʃˈemoˌiara pʰrtʰˈebitʰ 
ˈumodzrˌao ʃˈemdeɡ mˈan mˈoulˌodnelad mtrˈedi dˈainˌaxa da mˈoxutsʰma mpʰrˈinavi tʰˈevzi dˈainˌaxa 
ˈamovˌarda tsqlˈidan da sˈasotsˌarkvetʰˌili tsʰˈuravs zˈedapˌirze 

dˈelpʰini xmˈamaɣla tʰkʰvˈa mˈoxutsʰma dˈidi dˈelpʰini 

mˈan nˈitʃbebi ɡˈaɡzavna da mʃvˈildis kʰvˈemodan pˈatara xˈazi ɡˈamoˌiɣo ˈis 
hkʰˈonda mˈavtʰulis tsˈinamdzɣvˌari da sˈaʃuˌalo zˈomis kˈauci da sˈatquˌaras ˈertʰˈertʰi 
sˈardini mˈan ɡˈauʃva ɡvˈerdidan da ʃˈemdeɡ stsrˈapʰad mˈiiqvˌana ˈa

tʰˈevzi mˈoxutsʰi xˈedavda tsqˈalʃi mtsʰˈire ˈamobˌurtsʰulˌobas rˈomelitsʰ dˈidia

ˈacrida tsqˈals pʰrˈenis kʰvˈemotʰ tʰˈevzi da ˈikʰnebˌoda 
tsqˈali sˈitʃkʰaritʰ mˈimavˌali rˈodesatsʰ tʰˈevzi dˈaetsʰa dˈidi skˈolaa

mˈistʰvis da ˈisini dzˈalian stsrˈapʰad mˈidian 

ˈis ˈuqurˌebda mpʰrˈinav tʰˈevzs rˈomelitsʰ ˈisev da ˈisev ˈipʰetʰkʰˌebda da

tʰˈevzi sˈadme ˈunda ˈiqos

dzˈapʰebi mˈis ˈukan tsqˈalʃi ˈezos mˈidevs 

ˈaɡua mˈala tʰkʰvˈa kˈatsʰma ʃˈen mˈedzav

ʃˈemdeɡ mˈan dˈainˌaxa ˈertʰi zˈedapˌirze rˈomelitsʰ tsrˈeebʃi tsʰˈuravda mˈan ˈar dˈainˌaxa 
mˈeoris pʰˈarpʰli 

vˈer mˈovelˌodi mˈatʰ mˈokvlas ɡˈaipʰˌikʰra mˈan ʃˈemedzlo tʰˈavis drˈoze mkʰˈonoda 
mˈaɡram ˈorives mdzˈimed vˈatsqenˌine da vˈertsʰ ˈertʰi vˈer ɡrədznˈobs tʰˈavs kˈarɡad tʰˈu 
ʃˈemedzlo ɡˈamomˌeqenˌebina dʒˈoxi ˈori xˈelitʰ pˈirvelis mˈokvla ʃˈemedzlo 
ˈertʰi ˈautsʰˌileblad ˈaxlatsʰ ɡˈaipʰˌikʰra 

mˈas ˈar sˈurda tʰˈevzistʰvis ʃˈexedva mˈan ˈitsʰoda rˈom mˈisi nˈaxevˌari ˈiqo

zvˈiɡenˌebi 

mˈale dˈaɣamdˌeba tʰkʰvˈa mˈan mˈaʃin mˈe ˈunda dˈavinˌaxo mˈisi brətsqˈinvalˌeba 
hˈavana tʰˈu dzˈalian ʃˈors vˈikʰnebi ˈaɣmosˌavletʰˌisken dˈavinˌaxav ˈertʰis ʃˈukʰebs 
ˈaxali plˈaʒebis ʃˈesaxeb 

ˈaxla dzˈalian ʃˈors vˈer vˈikʰnebi ɡˈaipʰˌikʰra mˈan ˈimedˌia ˈartsʰ ˈaravin qˈopʰila 
ʃˈeʃpʰotʰˌebuli rˈa tʰkʰmˈa ˈunda mxˈolod bˈicia sˈanervˌiulo mˈaɡram dˈartsmunˌebuli vˈar ˈis

tqˈuilad ˈar ɡkʰˈondes ˈes ʃˈubi tʰˈavze

tʰˈavisˌupʰlad tsʰˈuravdnen mˈe ˈunda ˈamomˌecra kˈanonprˌoekʰti mˈatʰtʰan sˈabrdzolvˌelad
